MARKET DIRECTION: DOWN — NIFTY in a downtrend since Wed, 2 Sept 2026. No new buys (no futures, no calls): puts instead — 295 on 38 names (3 indices, 35 stocks). (close of Mon, 28 Sept 2026)
NIFTY closed at 22,780.25 on Mon, 28 Sept 2026; its 20-day average 23,447.42, its 50-day 23,963.37, its 200-day 24,406.9. A close under the 20-day average, with the 20-day under the 50-day, is what the desk’s trend reader (market-regime.js) calls a downtrend. It is read from completed daily closes, so it does not change during the day. That is the close of Mon, 28 Sept 2026: the close of Tue, 29 Sept 2026 was not on hand when this was built. Puts are looked for on NIFTY, BANKNIFTY, FINNIFTY, MIDCPNIFTY, NIFTYNXT50, and on the 45 stocks the desk rates SELL or REDUCE that are falling themselves (under their own 20-day and 50-day averages); 20 more are rated SELL or REDUCE but are not falling, so no put. 38 of those names had contracts that cleared every gate (indices: NIFTY, BANKNIFTY, MIDCPNIFTY). Tested first, and it FAILED: on five years of NSE’s own NIFTY data, selling NIFTY in these downtrends made +0.29% a trade in the final year — 18 trades, t = 0.205, no different from nothing (the test was fixed before it was run). These puts are here because you asked for the market’s direction, not because they are proven. Each is tracked on TRACK from the moment it is shown.

Puts — you BUY the put, it pays if the index or stock FALLS

These 295 rows are on 38 names: several strikes of one name are one bet, not several. Most rows are in: Index 123, Financial Services 67, Capital Goods 24. Rows in one sector tend to fall together. With no trend and fairly priced options, a put is expected to lose about its charges: about −₹30,393.04 across all 295 rows, one lot each, plus the bid-ask spread, which is not known without a live book (295 of these rows). 218 of 295 are priced above their own recent movement (implied above realised), which makes it worse. They make money only if the fall continues, and selling NIFTY in these downtrends failed its pre-registered test (final year t = 0.205).

For puts these figures are the OPTIMISTIC end. Each put is repriced with Black-76 at its own implied volatility, held where it is — but a put loses on the days the market rises, and on those days implied volatility usually falls, which takes still more off a put (India VIX moved −7% on Tue, 3 Feb 2026). The real loss on such a day can be larger than shown.

Hit rate counts only trades that filled and then finished. IF RANDOM is how often the target would come before the stop if prices moved at random — each trade's break-even, before charges. Only a hit rate above it, over enough separate bets, is evidence of an edge. The touch odds the desk showed ignore the stop, so they always run higher. Futures returns are GROSS per unit; options are NET of every charge per lot. ALL has no average return (futures move about 1.5%, options about 50%), and its money is options net a lot plus futures at 20 units, gross.

HOW THIS IS SCORED. Every price this page freezes is bought on paper at the moment it is shown. Each build then checks the market, using the day’s high and low so a move between two builds is not missed. Every ambiguity counts against the record: a target and a stop in the same window is a stop; a target in the same window as the fill is not counted; a gap through the stop exits at the open; a fill is always at the limit, never better; a price from before the level was shown can never fill it. Unfilled day orders die at 15:30. Futures are held at most 20 sessions; options until expiry. Stored in the private repository under docs/ledger/, committed on every build, and copied to the Google Sheet.
WHERE THESE PRICES COME FROM. All prices are from the 2026-09-29 settlement file. The market was in its pre-open when this was built, so nothing here is executable right now. Premiums and the futures prices they are measured against both come from that same settlement file, so they describe the same moment. Not checked against NSE on this build (before the open, NSE's quote pages still show the previous session). Broker login OK from the build server at 2026-09-30 00:56 IST — a NIFTY quote came back. F&O ban for 2026-09-30: SAIL — left out entirely, because NSE allows only exits in a banned stock. This page is a snapshot, rebuilt on a schedule — it is not a live feed. Check the price on your broker before placing anything. Net figures are after brokerage, STT at 0.15%, exchange, stamp and GST; the spread is extra and is only known where a live book was seen.
WHAT THIS DOES NOT ESTABLISH. That any of it makes money. The paper ledger has 205 resolved trade(s) so far — far too few for a measured hit rate — and every test fixed in advance so far, on data never used to build the rule, found no edge: the desk's own ranking (final year t = 0.75), 12-1 momentum, time-series momentum, low volatility, a 10-month market trend filter, and the NIFTY trend reader that now chooses between buys and puts (selling NIFTY in its downtrends: final year t = 0.21) — all six FAIL. The ranking is built on things that were measured, which is not the same as an edge that was proven. Size accordingly.
